Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on oceanography structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 632. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 66 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 632 + O(1/n^66). Summing over all modes and applying the oceanography convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
